function outingMatrixToObjectRows(matrix) {
  const rows = Array.isArray(matrix) ? matrix : [];
  const normal = matrixToObjectRows(rows);

  );
  if (hasUsableHeaders) return normal;

  // 2026-08 헤더 표준화 과정에서 이 탭은 1~2행 설명 다음 3행부터
  // 정규화 데이터가 직접 기록되는 구조가 되었습니다. 이 경우 헤더 자동감지 대신
  // 표준 컬럼 순서를 사용해 안전하게 객체화합니다.
  const keys = [
    "외출ID", "원본응답ID", "외출일자", "학생ID", "학년", "반", "번호", "성명",
    "신청구분", "외출구분", "운영형태", "외출시간", "복귀예정시간", "자택도착예정시간",
    "사유", "목적지", "보호자확인", "비고", "처리상태", "응답일시", "처리일시",
    "UEP전송상태", "UEP전송일시", "운영기간", "적용규칙ID", "규칙효과",
    "운영포함여부", "규칙판정", "판정사유", "규칙판정일시"
  ];
  return rows.slice(2)
    .filter((row) => Array.isArray(row) && row.some((value) => String(value ?? "").trim()))
    .filter((row) => {
      const first = String(row[0] ?? "").trim();
      const date = localDate(row[2]);
      const name = String(row[7] ?? "").trim();
      return /^DOUT-/i.test(first) || (date && name);
    })
    .map((row) => Object.fromEntries(keys.map((key, index) => [key, row[index] ?? ""])));
}

function serialToDate(value) {
  const serial = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(serial) || serial < 20000 || serial > 80000) return "";
  const utc = new Date(Math.round((serial - 25569) * 86400 * 1000));
  return `${utc.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(utc.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(utc.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function localDate(value) {
  if (value === "" || value == null) return "";
  if (value instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))
    return `${value.getFullYear()}-${String(value.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(value.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  const serialDate = serialToDate(value);
  if (serialDate) return serialDate;
  const text = String(value).trim();
  // __UEP_08159_SCHOOL_READ_DATE_CANONICAL__
  if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T/.test(text)){
    const instant=new Date(text);
    if(!Number.isNaN(instant.getTime())){
      const parts=new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA',{timeZone:'Asia/Seoul',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'}).formatToParts(instant);
      const p=Object.fromEntries(parts.map(part=>[part.type,part.value]));
      if(p.year&&p.month&&p.day)return p.year+'-'+p.month+'-'+p.day;
    }
  }
  const match = text.match(/(\d{4})[.\-/년]\s*(\d{1,2})[.\-/월]\s*(\d{1,2})/);
  if (match) return `${match[1]}-${match[2].padStart(2, "0")}-${match[3].padStart(2, "0")}`;
  const parsedText = new Date(text);
  if (!Number.isNaN(parsedText.getTime()) && /GMT|UTC|^[A-Za-z]{3}/.test(text))
    return `${parsedText.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(parsedText.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(parsedText.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  const compact=text.replace(/\D/g,'');
  if(/^20\d{6}$/.test(compact))return compact.slice(0,4)+'-'+compact.slice(4,6)+'-'+compact.slice(6,8);
  const monthDay=text.match(/(?:^|\D)(\d{1,2})\s*(?:월|[.\-/])\s*(\d{1,2})(?:\s*일)?(?:\D|$)/);
  if(monthDay)return '2026-'+monthDay[1].padStart(2,'0')+'-'+monthDay[2].padStart(2,'0');
  return text;
}

function localTime(value) {
  if (value === "" || value == null) return "";
  if (value instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))
    return `${String(value.getHours()).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(value.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  if (typeof value === "number") {
    const totalMinutes = Math.round((value % 1) * 24 * 60);
    return `${String(Math.floor(totalMinutes / 60) % 24).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(totalMinutes % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  }
  const text = String(value).trim();
  if (/^0\.\d+$/.test(text)) return localTime(Number(text));
  const match = text.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  return match ? `${match[1].padStart(2, "0")}:${match[2]}` : text;
}

function classNumberSafe(value) { return String(value || "").replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""); }

function normalizeHeaderText(value) {
  return String(value ?? "")
    .replace(/\r?\n/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();
}

function detectHeaderRowIndex(rows) {
  const source = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
  const scanLimit = Math.min(source.length, 12);
  let bestIndex = -1;
  let bestScore = -Infinity;

  for (let index = 0; index < scanLimit; index += 1) {
    const cells = (source[index] || []).map(normalizeHeaderText);
    const nonEmpty = cells.filter(Boolean);
    if (nonEmpty.length < 2) continue;

    const uniqueCount = new Set(nonEmpty).size;
    const duplicatePenalty = nonEmpty.length - uniqueCount;
    const knownHeaderHits = nonEmpty.filter((value) =>
      /^(학생ID|학번|성명|사용성명|학년|반|번호|일자|운영일|일정ID|프로그램ID|강좌ID|기록ID|상태|구분|설정키|파일구분|스프레드시트ID|탭명|탭용도)$/.test(value),
    ).length;
    const titlePenalty = nonEmpty.length <= 2 && /UEP|표준|시트|DB|관리|기준/.test(nonEmpty.join(" ")) ? 8 : 0;
    const score = nonEmpty.length * 3 + uniqueCount + knownHeaderHits * 8 - duplicatePenalty * 2 - titlePenalty;

    if (score > bestScore) {
      bestScore = score;
      bestIndex = index;
    }
  }

  return bestIndex;
}

function headerLookupKey(value) {
  return normalizeHeaderText(value).replace(/[\s·_\-()\[\]{}]/g, "").toLowerCase();
}

function valueByHeader(row, ...aliases) {
  if (!row || typeof row !== "object") return "";
  for (const alias of aliases) {
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(row, alias)) return row[alias];
  }
  const wanted = new Set(aliases.map(headerLookupKey));
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(row)) {
    const normalizedKey = headerLookupKey(key);
    if (wanted.has(normalizedKey)) return value;
    // 병합형 2단 헤더(예: "학생정보 학번", "외출정보 외출일자")도
    // 하위 헤더 별칭으로 안전하게 찾습니다.
    for (const aliasKey of wanted) {
      if (aliasKey && normalizedKey.endsWith(aliasKey)) return value;
    }
  }
  return "";
}

function looksLikeSubHeaderRow(row) {
  const cells = (Array.isArray(row) ? row : []).map(normalizeHeaderText);
  const nonEmpty = cells.filter(Boolean);
  if (nonEmpty.length < 2) return false;
  const headerTerms = nonEmpty.filter((value) =>
    /^(?:원점수|표준점수|백분위|등급|과목|학생ID|학번|성명|이름|시험명|시험월|시험년도|학년도|반|번호|일자|날짜|외출일자|외출시간|복귀예정시간|귀사예정시간|자택도착예정시간|사유|목적지|구분|상태|처리상태|승인자|비고)$/.test(value),
  ).length;
  const numericLike = nonEmpty.filter((value) => /^[-+]?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?$/.test(value)).length;
  return headerTerms >= 2 || (headerTerms >= 1 && numericLike === 0);
}

function composeTwoLevelHeaders(parentRow, childRow) {
  const parents = (Array.isArray(parentRow) ? parentRow : []).map(normalizeHeaderText);
  const children = (Array.isArray(childRow) ? childRow : []).map(normalizeHeaderText);
  const width = Math.max(parents.length, children.length);
  const result = [];
  let activeParent = "";
  for (let index = 0; index < width; index += 1) {
    const parent = parents[index] || "";
    const child = children[index] || "";
    if (parent) activeParent = parent;
    if (child) {
      const parentKey = headerLookupKey(activeParent);
      const childKey = headerLookupKey(child);
      result.push(parentKey && parentKey !== childKey ? `${activeParent} ${child}`.trim() : child);
    } else {
      result.push(parent || activeParent || `__EMPTY_${index + 1}`);
    }
  }
  return result;
}

function uniqueHeaders(headers) {
  const seen = new Map();
  return headers.map((header, index) => {
    const base = normalizeHeaderText(header) || `__EMPTY_${index + 1}`;
    const count = (seen.get(base) || 0) + 1;
    seen.set(base, count);
    return count === 1 ? base : `${base}__${count}`;
  });
}

  // 운영일을 함께 표시할 수 있습니다. 기존 응답은 원문 그대로 보존하고, 매칭 단계에서만

  const stripProgramDisplayDate = value => String(value || "")

    .replace(/\s*[（(\[]\s*(?:20\d{2}[.\-/년]\s*)?\d{1,2}[.\-/월]\s*\d{1,2}(?:[.\-/일]\s*)?(?:[~～\-–—]\s*(?:20\d{2}[.\-/년]\s*)?\d{1,2}[.\-/월]\s*\d{1,2}(?:[.\-/일]\s*)?)?\s*[）)\]]\s*$/u, "")

  const programSemester = program => {

  };

    if(pid && reportPid && pid===reportPid)return true;

  const auth = await getReadonlySheetsAuth(credentials);

  const entries = Object.entries(SHEET_RANGES);
  const matrices = {};
  // 기본정보 연결시트를 가장 먼저, 작은 묶음으로 읽습니다.

  const chunkSize = 12;
  const chunks = [];
  for (let offset = 0; offset < entries.length; offset += chunkSize) {
    chunks.push({ offset, chunk: entries.slice(offset, offset + chunkSize) });
  }
  await mapWithConcurrency(chunks, 1, async ({ offset, chunk }) => {
    try {
      const valueRanges = await readSheetBatch(token, UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID, chunk.map(([, range]) => range));
      valueRanges.forEach((item, index) => {
        const fallbackName = chunk[index]?.[0];
        matrices[fallbackName] = item.values || [];
      });
    } catch (chunkError) {
      console.warn(`[UEP] 기본정보 묶음 조회 실패(${offset + 1}~${offset + chunk.length}), 개별 재시도`, chunkError?.message || chunkError);
      await mapWithConcurrency(chunk, 1, async ([logicalName, range]) => {
        try {
          const one = await readSheetBatch(token, UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID, [range]);
          matrices[logicalName] = one?.[0]?.values || [];
        } catch (sheetError) {
          matrices[logicalName] = [];
          console.warn(`[UEP] 기본정보 탭 조회 실패: ${logicalName}`, sheetError?.message || sheetError);
        }
      });

  await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'${target}'!A3:X${rows.length+3}`,[required,...rows]);
  console.log(`[UEP] 선택보고서 정규화 자동 복구 완료: ${rows.length}건 · 학생확인 ${unmatchedStudents}건`);
  return {ok:true,repaired:true,count:rows.length,unmatchedStudents};
}

async function fetchLiveData({ force = false, credentials = null } = {}) {
  if (!force && liveDataCache && Date.now() - liveDataFetchedAt < 120000) return liveDataCache;
  // 0.81.40: 관리자 로컬 서비스계정과 담임 School Read API 세션을 동일 읽기 파이프라인으로 연결합니다.
  const auth = await getReadonlySheetsAuth(credentials);
  const token = auth.token;
  const entries = Object.entries(SHEET_RANGES);
  const matrices = {};
  // 기본정보 연결시트를 가장 먼저, 작은 묶음으로 읽습니다.
  // 탭 추가로 URL이 길어지거나 특정 탭이 잠시 실패해도 전체 연결이 끊기지 않도록 합니다.
  const chunkSize = 12;
  const chunks = [];
  for (let offset = 0; offset < entries.length; offset += chunkSize) {
    chunks.push({ offset, chunk: entries.slice(offset, offset + chunkSize) });
  }
  await mapWithConcurrency(chunks, 1, async ({ offset, chunk }) => {
    try {
      const valueRanges = await readSheetBatch(token, UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID, chunk.map(([, range]) => range));
      valueRanges.forEach((item, index) => {
        const fallbackName = chunk[index]?.[0];
        matrices[fallbackName] = item.values || [];
      });
    } catch (chunkError) {
      console.warn(`[UEP] 기본정보 묶음 조회 실패(${offset + 1}~${offset + chunk.length}), 개별 재시도`, chunkError?.message || chunkError);
      await mapWithConcurrency(chunk, 1, async ([logicalName, range]) => {
        try {
          const one = await readSheetBatch(token, UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID, [range]);
          matrices[logicalName] = one?.[0]?.values || [];
        } catch (sheetError) {
          matrices[logicalName] = [];
          console.warn(`[UEP] 기본정보 탭 조회 실패: ${logicalName}`, sheetError?.message || sheetError);
        }
      });
    }
  });
  entries.forEach(([name]) => { if (!matrices[name]) matrices[name] = []; });

  // 선택보고서 정규화 탭의 구조가 손상된 경우 구글폼 원본으로 복구한 뒤 읽습니다.
  // 읽기 전용 담임 세션에서는 정규화 탭 자동복구(쓰기)를 실행하지 않습니다.
  if(auth.mode!=='school_read_api') await ensureSelectedReportNormalization(token);

  // 학생응답 가공 결과: 현재 실제 존재하는 데이터 처리 탭만 직접 읽습니다.
  // 선택과목은 응답수집 06_선택과목응답을 별도 읽고 기존 fallback 로직으로 구성합니다.
  const processingRanges = [
    ["02_학사외출_일자별", "'02_학사외출_일자별'!A1:AD10000"],
    ["20_공통보고서_정규화", "'20_공통보고서_정규화'!A1:X5000"],
    ["21_선택보고서_정규화", "'21_선택보고서_정규화'!A1:X5000"],
    ["30_야자출결_정규화", "'30_야자출결_정규화'!A1:P70000"],
    // 학교캘린더는 기본정보 연결시트가 아니라 데이터 처리시트의 직접 이관값만 사용합니다.
    ["41_학교캘린더", "'41_학교캘린더'!A1:W3000"],
  ];
  try {
    const result = await readSheetBatch(token, UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID, processingRanges.map(([,range])=>range));
    processingRanges.forEach(([logicalName],index)=>{matrices[logicalName]=result?.[index]?.values||[];});
  } catch(error) {
    // 한 탭의 일시 오류가 보고서·학사외출·선택과목 등 정상 탭까지 모두 비우지 않도록
    // 묶음 조회 실패 시 각 탭을 독립적으로 재시도합니다. 평상시에는 1회 batchGet으로 quota를 보호합니다.
    console.warn("[UEP] 처리시트 묶음 조회 실패 - 개별 복구 시도",error?.message||error);
    await mapWithConcurrency(processingRanges,1,async([logicalName,range])=>{
      try{const one=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);matrices[logicalName]=one?.[0]?.values||[];}
      catch(sheetError){matrices[logicalName]=[];console.warn(`[UEP] 처리시트 개별 조회 실패: ${logicalName}`,sheetError?.message||sheetError);}
    });
  }

  // 학사외출은 데이터 처리시트의 02_학사외출_일자별만 조회합니다.
  // 응답 원본이나 기본정보 연결시트, 별도 운영 시트를 직접 참조하지 않습니다.

  // 입력센터 원본은 별도 [UEP] 입력센터 원본 스프레드시트에서 읽습니다.
  try {
    const inputResult=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'02_자료원본'!A1:X10000"]);
    matrices["__INPUT_CENTER_SOURCE"]=inputResult?.[0]?.values||[];
  } catch(error) {
    matrices["__INPUT_CENTER_SOURCE"]=[];
    console.warn("[UEP] 입력센터 원본 조회 실패",error?.message||error);
  }

  // 학생이 직접 입력한 희망계열·희망전공은 정규화 자료의 유무와 관계없이 항상 읽습니다.
  // 선택과목 목록은 처리시트/고교학점제 결과를 우선하되, 진로 보조정보는 원응답의 최신값을 기준으로 합니다.
  try {
    const rawSelection = await readSheetBatch(token, "1K-Me8Pm8T2d5fGHoqSPEAu4RXozETUQ-N5pZVxjs8Hw", ["'06_선택과목응답'!A1:U2000"]);
    matrices["__SELECTION_RESPONSES"] = rawSelection?.[0]?.values || [];
  } catch (error) {
    matrices["__SELECTION_RESPONSES"] = [];
    console.warn("[UEP] 선택과목 응답 조회 실패", error?.message || error);
  }

  // 규칙 적용 자료: 평상시는 batchGet, 한 탭이라도 실패하면 각 탭을 독립 재조회합니다.
  // 특히 40_학사외출규칙은 다른 규칙 탭 오류와 무관하게 반드시 복구를 시도합니다.
  const ruleEntries = Object.entries(RULE_SHEET_RANGES);
  try {
    const ruleValueRanges = await readSheetBatch(token, UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID, ruleEntries.map(([, range]) => range));
    ruleValueRanges.forEach((item, index) => {
      const logicalName = ruleEntries[index]?.[0];
      matrices[logicalName] = item.values || [];
    });
    ruleEntries.forEach(([name]) => { if (!matrices[name]) matrices[name] = []; });
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn("[UEP] 규칙로그 묶음 조회 실패 - 개별 복구 시도", error?.message || error);
    await mapWithConcurrency(ruleEntries, 1, async ([logicalName, range]) => {
      try {
        const one = await readSheetBatch(token, UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID, [range]);
        matrices[logicalName] = one?.[0]?.values || [];
      } catch (sheetError) {
        matrices[logicalName] = [];
        console.warn(`[UEP] 규칙로그 개별 조회 실패: ${logicalName}`, sheetError?.message || sheetError);
      }
    });
  }
  // v0.76.93: 학사외출 규칙은 화면 운영의 핵심이므로 규칙 묶음 결과와 무관하게 마지막에 단독 재조회합니다.
  // batchGet의 일부 누락/순서 문제 또는 다른 규칙 탭 오류가 있어도 40_학사외출규칙 원본을 우선합니다.
  try {

      token,

    const dormRuleMatrix = dormRuleResult?.[0]?.values || [];
    if (dormRuleMatrix.length >= 3) {

    } else {
      console.warn("[UEP] 40_학사외출규칙 단독 조회 결과가 비어 있습니다.");
    }
  } catch (dormRuleError) {
    console.warn("[UEP] 40_학사외출규칙 단독 조회 실패", dormRuleError?.message || dormRuleError);
  }

  const curriculumSpreadsheetId = "1fevlAWQZqWQkiYl-eGRCdzidBj59Z8NqOxfCrGvwlHI";
const curriculumReads = [
  ["__CURRICULUM_09", "'09_수강신청결과'!A1:AG1200"],

  ["__CURRICULUM_STUDENTS", "'02_학생마스터'!A1:AA1200"]
];
for (const [logicalName, range] of curriculumReads) {
  try {

    matrices[logicalName] = one?.[0]?.values || [];

  }
}

  // 서비스 계정에 열람 권한이 없거나 일부 탭 형식이 달라도 UEP 전체 동기화는 계속됩니다.

      };

  });
  

function inputCenterNatureToStoredType(nature=""){
  const n=String(nature||"");
  if(n==="보고서")return "보고서·탐구보고서";
  if(n==="신청")return "프로그램 신청서";
  if(n==="만족도조사")return "만족도·설문조사";
  if(n==="출결·명단")return "출결·참여명단";
  return n||"기타";
}
function inputCenterProgramIdRaw(id=""){return String(id||"").replace(/^(common|selected)-/,'');}
function inputCenterSemester(value="",submittedAt=""){
  const t=String(value||""); if(/(^|[^0-9])1([^0-9]|$)|1학기/.test(t))return "1학기"; if(/(^|[^0-9])2([^0-9]|$)|2학기/.test(t))return "2학기";
  const m=String(submittedAt||"").match(/\d{4}[-/.](\d{1,2})/);return m&&Number(m[1])<=7?"1학기":"2학기";
}
async function classifyInputCenterBatch(payload={}){
  try{
    const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());if(!validateServiceAccount(account))throw new Error('Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.');
    const batchId=String(payload.batchId||'').trim();if(!batchId)throw new Error('입력 배치를 선택해 주세요.');
    const token=await getSheetsToken(account);const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'02_자료원본'!A3:X10000"]);const rows=(result?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);
    let count=0;const storedType=inputCenterNatureToStoredType(payload.nature);const pid=String(payload.programId||'');const pk=String(payload.programKind||'');const note=pid?`ROUTE:PROGRAM_REPORT:${pid}`:'';const writes=[];
    for(let i=0;i<rows.length;i++)if(String(rows[i]?.[1]||'')===batchId){const sheetRow=i+4;const current=Array.from({length:24},(_,j)=>rows[i]?.[j]??'');current[3]=storedType;current[4]=payload.nature==='보고서'?'보고서 제출':payload.nature;let meta={};try{meta=JSON.parse(String(current[16]||''));}catch{}current[6]=inputCenterSemester(payload.programSemester||'',meta.submittedAt||'');current[17]='검수완료';current[19]=pid;current[20]=pk;current[23]=note||current[23]||'';writes.push({range:`'02_자료원본'!A${sheetRow}:X${sheetRow}`,values:[current]});count++;}
    await batchUpdateSheetValues(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,writes);
    liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true,count,batchId};
  }catch(error){return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||String(error)};}
}
async function normalizeInputCenterBatch(payload={}){
  try{
    const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());if(!validateServiceAccount(account))throw new Error('Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.');
    const records=Array.isArray(payload.records)?payload.records:[];if(!records.length)throw new Error('정규화할 자료가 없습니다.');
    if(String(payload.nature||'')!=='보고서')throw new Error('현재 정규화 자동적용은 보고서부터 지원합니다. 신청·만족도·명단은 다음 라우팅 단계에서 연결합니다.');
    const kind=String(payload.programKind||'')==='selected'?'selected':'common';
    const targetSheet=kind==='selected'?'21_선택보고서_정규화':'20_공통보고서_정규화';
    const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
    // REPORT_TEXT_V2 표준 스키마 A:X. 원본유형/원본ID로 출처를 구분하므로 입력센터 자료도 기존 데이터 바로 다음 행에 연속 저장합니다.
    const DATA_START_ROW=4, INPUT_END_ROW=5000;
    const existingResult=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID,[`'${targetSheet}'!A3:X${INPUT_END_ROW}`]);
    const matrix=existingResult?.[0]?.values||[];

function inputCenterNatureToStoredType(nature=""){
  const n=String(nature||"");
  if(n==="보고서")return "보고서·탐구보고서";
  if(n==="신청")return "프로그램 신청서";
  if(n==="만족도조사")return "만족도·설문조사";
  if(n==="출결·명단")return "출결·참여명단";
  return n||"기타";
}
function inputCenterProgramIdRaw(id=""){return String(id||"").replace(/^(common|selected)-/,'');}
function inputCenterSemester(value="",submittedAt=""){
  const t=String(value||""); if(/(^|[^0-9])1([^0-9]|$)|1학기/.test(t))return "1학기"; if(/(^|[^0-9])2([^0-9]|$)|2학기/.test(t))return "2학기";
  const m=String(submittedAt||"").match(/\d{4}[-/.](\d{1,2})/);return m&&Number(m[1])<=7?"1학기":"2학기";
}
async function classifyInputCenterBatch(payload={}){
  try{
    const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());if(!validateServiceAccount(account))throw new Error('Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.');
    const batchId=String(payload.batchId||'').trim();if(!batchId)throw new Error('입력 배치를 선택해 주세요.');
    const token=await getSheetsToken(account);const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'02_자료원본'!A3:X10000"]);const rows=(result?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);
    let count=0;const storedType=inputCenterNatureToStoredType(payload.nature);const pid=String(payload.programId||'');const pk=String(payload.programKind||'');const note=pid?`ROUTE:PROGRAM_REPORT:${pid}`:'';const writes=[];

    liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true,count,batchId};
  }catch(error){return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||String(error)};}
}

async function refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved){
  if(!saved?.refresh_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  const clientId=await loadGoogleOAuthClientId();
  if(String(saved.client_id||'').trim() && String(saved.client_id).trim()!==clientId){
    await disconnectGoogleUser();
    throw googleOAuthError('Google 연결 설정이 갱신되었습니다. Google 계정 연결을 한 번만 다시 승인해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
  }
  const body=new URLSearchParams({client_id:clientId,refresh_token:String(saved.refresh_token),grant_type:'refresh_token'});
  const response=await net.fetch('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'},body:body.toString()});
  if(!response.ok){
    let payload={},detail='',errCode='';
    try{payload=await response.json();detail=String(payload?.error_description||'');errCode=String(payload?.error||'');}catch{}
    if(errCode==='invalid_client'||errCode==='invalid_grant'){
      googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId:'',expiresAt:0};
      await disconnectGoogleUser();
      throw googleOAuthError('저장된 Google 인증정보가 현재 UEP 연결설정과 맞지 않습니다. Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
    }
    throw googleOAuthError(`Google 계정 토큰 갱신 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:''}`,"UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  }
  const next=await response.json();
  const merged={...saved,...next,client_id:clientId,expires_at:Date.now()+Math.max(60,Number(next.expires_in||3600))*1000,updated_at:new Date().toISOString()};
  await saveGoogleUserOAuth(merged);
  return merged;
}
async function getGoogleUserSheetsToken(){
  let saved=await readGoogleUserOAuth();
  if(!saved) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결이 필요합니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  if(saved.access_token && Number(saved.expires_at||0)>Date.now()+90000) return String(saved.access_token);
  saved=await refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved);
  if(!saved.access_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 액세스 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  return String(saved.access_token);
}
async function getReadonlySheetsAuth(credentials=null){
  if(credentials){if(!validateServiceAccount(credentials))throw new Error("Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");return {token:await getSheetsToken(credentials),mode:'service_account',account:credentials.client_email||''};}
  const session=await readSchoolReadSession();
  if(session?.token&&session?.deviceId)return {token:'UEP_SCHOOL_READ:'+session.token,mode:'school_read_api',account:String(session?.user?.email||'')};
  throw schoolReadError('교사 이름과 이메일로 UEP 로그인 후 학교 데이터를 불러올 수 있습니다.','UEP_SCHOOL_READ_LOGIN_REQUIRED');
}
async function authorizeGoogleUser({loginHint=''}={}){
  const clientId=await loadGoogleOAuthClientId();
  const verifier=crypto.randomBytes(48).toString('base64url');
  const challenge=crypto.createHash('sha256').update(verifier).digest('base64url');
  const state=crypto.randomBytes(24).toString('base64url');
  let timer=null;
  let settled=false;
  let callbackResolve,callbackReject;
  const callbackPromise=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{callbackResolve=resolve;callbackReject=reject;});
  const server=http.createServer((req,res)=>{
    try{
      const u=new URL(req.url||'/', 'http://127.0.0.1');
      if(u.pathname!=='/' && u.pathname!=='/oauth2callback'){res.writeHead(404);res.end('Not found');return;}
      const returnedState=String(u.searchParams.get('state')||'');
      const code=String(u.searchParams.get('code')||'');
      const error=String(u.searchParams.get('error')||'');
      res.writeHead(200,{'content-type':'text/html; charset=utf-8','cache-control':'no-store'});
      if(error||returnedState!==state||!code){
        res.end('<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><title>UEP Google 연결</title><h2>UEP Google 계정 연결이 취소되었거나 실패했습니다.</h2><p>이 창을 닫고 UEP에서 다시 시도해 주세요.</p>');
        if(!settled){settled=true;callbackReject(googleOAuthError(error?`Google 승인 취소: ${error}`:'Google 승인 응답 검증에 실패했습니다.','UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_CALLBACK_FAILED'));}
        return;
      }
      res.end('<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><title>UEP Google 연결</title><h2>Google 승인 정보가 UEP로 전달되었습니다.</h2><p>이 창을 닫고 UEP로 돌아가 연결 완료 여부를 확인해 주세요.</p>');
      if(!settled){settled=true;callbackResolve(code);}
    }catch(error){if(!settled){settled=true;callbackReject(error);}}
  });
  try{
    await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{server.once('error',reject);server.listen(0,'127.0.0.1',resolve);});
    const address=server.address();
    const port=typeof address==='object'&&address?address.port:0;
    if(!port) throw googleOAuthError('Google 승인용 로컬 포트를 열지 못했습니다.','UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_CALLBACK_FAILED');
    const redirectUri=`http://127.0.0.1:${port}`;
    const authUrl=new URL('https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/v2/auth');
    authUrl.searchParams.set('client_id',clientId);
    authUrl.searchParams.set('redirect_uri',redirectUri);
    authUrl.searchParams.set('response_type','code');
    authUrl.searchParams.set('scope','openid email https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets.readonly');
    authUrl.searchParams.set('code_challenge',challenge);
    authUrl.searchParams.set('code_challenge_method','S256');
    authUrl.searchParams.set('state',state);
    authUrl.searchParams.set('access_type','offline');
    authUrl.searchParams.set('prompt','consent');
    if(String(loginHint||'').includes('@')) authUrl.searchParams.set('login_hint',String(loginHint).trim());
    await shell.openExternal(authUrl.toString());
    timer=setTimeout(()=>{if(!settled){settled=true;callbackReject(googleOAuthError('Google 계정 승인이 시간 초과되었습니다.','UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_TIMEOUT'));}},180000);
    const code=await callbackPromise;
    const body=new URLSearchParams();
    body.set('client_id',clientId);

    body.set('code',code);
    body.set('code_verifier',verifier);
    body.set('redirect_uri',redirectUri);
    body.set('grant_type','authorization_code');
    const response=await net.fetch('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded;charset=UTF-8','accept':'application/json'},body:body.toString()});
    if(!response.ok){
      let errCode='',detail='',raw='';
      try{const payload=await response.json();errCode=String(payload?.error||'');detail=String(payload?.error_description||'');raw=JSON.stringify(payload);}catch{try{raw=await response.text();}catch{}}
      const suffix=[errCode,detail].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
      if(errCode==='invalid_client'){
        googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId:'',expiresAt:0};
        await disconnectGoogleUser();
        throw googleOAuthError('Google OAuth 토큰 교환이 거부되었습니다. UEP의 Desktop OAuth Client ID와 Google 승인 설정을 확인한 뒤 다시 연결해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
      }
      throw googleOAuthError(`Google 토큰 교환 실패 (${response.status})${suffix?`: ${suffix}`:''}${!suffix&&raw?`: ${raw}`:''}`,'UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_TOKEN_FAILED');
    }
    const tokenPayload=await response.json();
    if(!tokenPayload.access_token) throw googleOAuthError('Google 액세스 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.','UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_TOKEN_FAILED');
    let info={};
    try{info=await googleUserInfo(tokenPayload.access_token);}catch{}
    let idTokenInfo={};
    try{
      const parts=String(tokenPayload.id_token||'').split('.');
      if(parts.length>=2) idTokenInfo=JSON.parse(Buffer.from(parts[1].replace(/-/g,'+').replace(/_/g,'/'),'base64').toString('utf8'))||{};
    }catch{}
    const fallbackEmail=String(loginHint||'').trim();
    const saved={...tokenPayload,client_id:clientId,email:String(info?.email||idTokenInfo?.email||fallbackEmail||''),sub:String(info?.sub||idTokenInfo?.sub||''),expires_at:Date.now()+Math.max(60,Number(tokenPayload.expires_in||3600))*1000,created_at:new Date().toISOString(),updated_at:new Date().toISOString()};
    const persistResult=await saveGoogleUserOAuth(saved);
    const verifySaved=await readGoogleUserOAuth();
    if(!verifySaved?.access_token) throw googleOAuthError('Google 승인 토큰 저장 확인에 실패했습니다.','UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_PERSIST_FAILED');
    liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;
    let syncOk=false, syncError='', syncCode='', data=null;
    try {
      data=await fetchLiveData({force:true});
      syncOk=true;
    } catch(error) {
      syncError=String(error?.message||error||'Google 시트 조회 실패');
      syncCode=String(error?.code||'');
    }
    return {ok:true,authorized:true,mode:'user_oauth',account:saved.email,sourceName:data?.sourceName||UEP_SPREADSHEET_NAME,preview:data?.summary||null,syncedAt:data?.syncedAt||'',syncOk,syncError,syncCode};
  }finally{
    if(timer)clearTimeout(timer);
    try{server.close();}catch{}
  }
}

async function disconnectGoogleUser(){
  for(const file of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalPath(),googleUserOAuthCanonicalRecoveryPath(),googleUserOAuthPath(),googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath()]){
    try{await fs.unlink(file);}catch(error){if(error?.code!=='ENOENT')throw error;}
  }
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true};
}
async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
  const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
    iss: credentials.client_email,
    scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets",
    aud: credentials.token_uri,
    iat: now,
    exp: now + 3600,
  }));
  const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
  const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
    assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
  });
  const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body,
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
  return payload.access_token;
}

async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
  if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
  url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
}

async function updateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values, attempt=0) {
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}`);
  url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "PUT",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail=""; try{ detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""; }catch{}
    if ((response.status===429 || response.status>=500) && attempt<4) {
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return updateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,range,values,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

async function batchUpdateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, data, attempt=0) {
  const items=(Array.isArray(data)?data:[]).filter(x=>x&&x.range&&Array.isArray(x.values));
  if(!items.length)return {totalUpdatedRows:0};
  const url=new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchUpdate`);
  const response=await fetch(url,{
    method:"POST",
    headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,"Content-Type":"application/json"},
    body:JSON.stringify({valueInputOption:"USER_ENTERED",data:items.map(x=>({range:x.range,majorDimension:"ROWS",values:x.values}))}),
  });
  if(!response.ok){
    let detail="";try{detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||"";}catch{}
    if((response.status===429||response.status>=500)&&attempt<4){
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return batchUpdateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,items,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 일괄 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}


function columnLetter(index) {
  let n=Number(index)+1, out="";
  while(n>0){ const r=(n-1)%26; out=String.fromCharCode(65+r)+out; n=Math.floor((n-1)/26); }
  return out;
}
function normalizeSheetHeader(value){return String(value??"").replace(/\r?\n/g," ").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();}
function findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,required){
  const wanted=required.map(x=>String(x));
  const limit=Math.min(Array.isArray(matrix)?matrix.length:0,12);
  for(let i=0;i<limit;i+=1){
    const row=(matrix[i]||[]).map(normalizeSheetHeader);
    if(wanted.every(name=>row.includes(name))) return {index:i,headers:row};
  }
  return null;
}

  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
  const range="'04_학생연락식별정보'!A1:Z1000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
  const header=findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,["학생ID"]);
  if(!header) throw new Error("04_학생연락식별정보의 헤더에서 학생ID를 찾지 못했습니다.");
  const studentId=String(payload.studentId||"").trim();
  if(!studentId) throw new Error("학생ID가 없어 저장할 수 없습니다.");
  const idCol=header.headers.indexOf("학생ID");
  let fieldCol=-1;
  for(const name of allowed[field]){ const idx=header.headers.indexOf(name); if(idx>=0){fieldCol=idx;break;} }
  if(fieldCol<0) throw new Error(`04_학생연락식별정보에 ${allowed[field][0]} 열이 없습니다.`);
  let dataRow=-1;
  for(let r=header.index+1;r<matrix.length;r+=1){ if(String(matrix[r]?.[idCol]||"").trim()===studentId){dataRow=r;break;} }
  const value=String(payload.value??"").trim();
  if(dataRow<0){
    const row=Array(Math.max(header.headers.length,fieldCol+1)).fill("");
    row[idCol]=studentId; row[fieldCol]=value;
    const useCol=header.headers.indexOf("현재사용여부"); if(useCol>=0)row[useCol]="Y";
    await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'04_학생연락식별정보'!A:Z",[row]);
  }else{
    const sheetRow=dataRow+1;
    const col=columnLetter(fieldCol);
    await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'04_학생연락식별정보'!${col}${sheetRow}:${col}${sheetRow}`,[[value]]);
  }
  liveDataCache=null; liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  const data=await fetchLiveData({force:true});
  return {ok:true,data};
}

  const allowed={phone:["학생연락처"],email:["학생이메일","이메일","이메일주소"],guardian1Phone:["보호자1연락처"],guardian2Phone:["보호자2연락처"]};
  const field=String(payload.field||"");
  if(!allowed[field]) throw new Error("수정할 수 없는 학생정보 항목입니다.");
  const role=String(payload.role||"");
  if(!["admin","homeroom"].includes(role)) throw new Error("학생 연락정보 수정 권한이 없습니다.");
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
  const range="'04_학생연락식별정보'!A1:Z1000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
  const header=findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,["학생ID"]);
  if(!header) throw new Error("04_학생연락식별정보의 헤더에서 학생ID를 찾지 못했습니다.");
  const studentId=String(payload.studentId||"").trim();
  if(!studentId) throw new Error("학생ID가 없어 저장할 수 없습니다.");
  const idCol=header.headers.indexOf("학생ID");
  let fieldCol=-1;
  for(const name of allowed[field]){ const idx=header.headers.indexOf(name); if(idx>=0){fieldCol=idx;break;} }
  if(fieldCol<0) throw new Error(`04_학생연락식별정보에 ${allowed[field][0]} 열이 없습니다.`);
  let dataRow=-1;

  const value=String(payload.value??"").trim();
  if(dataRow<0){
    const row=Array(Math.max(header.headers.length,fieldCol+1)).fill("");
    row[idCol]=studentId; row[fieldCol]=value;
    const useCol=header.headers.indexOf("현재사용여부"); if(useCol>=0)row[useCol]="Y";
    await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'04_학생연락식별정보'!A:Z",[row]);

    const sheetRow=dataRow+1;
    const col=columnLetter(fieldCol);

  }
  liveDataCache=null; liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  const data=await fetchLiveData({force:true});
  return {ok:true,data};
}

async function clearSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range) {
  const url = `https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}:clear`;
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: "{}",
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공결 삭제 실패 (${response.status})`);
  return response.json();
}

function officialAttendanceRow(payload={}, id="") {
  const now=new Date().toISOString();
  const type=String(payload.detailType||"결석");
  const start=String(payload.startPeriod||"");
  const end=String(payload.endPeriod||"");
  const unit=(start||end)?"교시":"일일";
  const scope=(start||end)?(start&&end?`${start}~${end}교시`:`${start||end}교시`):"하루 전체";
  return [[
    id,
    String(payload.studentId||""), String(payload.studentNo||""), String(payload.name||""),
    String(payload.grade||"1"), String(payload.className||payload.classNo||""), String(payload.date||""),
    String(payload.attendanceType||"출석인정"), type, start, end,
    String(payload.reasonText||payload.reason||""), String(payload.place||""),
    String(payload.evidenceRequired||"N"), String(payload.evidence||"불필요"),
    String(payload.signatureRequired||"N"), String(payload.signatureStatus||"불필요"),
    String(payload.registrar||"UEP 직접등록"), now, String(payload.editor||"UEP"), String(payload.note||""),

async function saveOfficialAttendance(payload={}) {
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
  const range="'30_공결기록'!A3:Z3000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
  const rows=matrix.slice(1);
  let id=String(payload.id||"").trim();
  let sheetRow=-1;
  if(id){
    const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===id);
    if(pos>=0) sheetRow=pos+4;
  }else{
    let max=0;
    rows.forEach(row=>{const m=String(row?.[0]||"").match(/^ATT-2026-(\\d+)$/);if(m)max=Math.max(max,Number(m[1]));});
    id=`ATT-2026-${String(max+1).padStart(4,"0")}`;
  }
  const values=officialAttendanceRow(payload,id);
  if(sheetRow>0) await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'30_공결기록'!A${sheetRow}:Z${sheetRow}`,values);
  else await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'30_공결기록'!A:Z",values);
  liveDataCache=null; liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  const data=await fetchLiveData({force:true});
  return {ok:true,id,data};
}

async function deleteOfficialAttendance(id="") {
  const key=String(id||"").trim();
  if(!key) throw new Error("삭제할 공결 ID가 없습니다.");
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'30_공결기록'!A3:Z3000"]);
  const rows=(result?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);
  const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===key);
  if(pos<0) throw new Error("삭제할 공결 기록을 찾지 못했습니다.");
  const sheetRow=pos+4;
  await clearSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'30_공결기록'!A${sheetRow}:Z${sheetRow}`);
  liveDataCache=null; liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  const data=await fetchLiveData({force:true});
  return {ok:true,data};
}

function dormRuleDateValue(value){
  if(value===null||value===undefined||value==='')return '';
  const raw=String(value).trim();
  if(/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(raw)){
    const serial=Number(raw);
    if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000){
      const d=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+Math.round(serial*86400000));
      if(!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())){
        const y=d.getUTCFullYear(),mo=String(d.getUTCMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),day=String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2,'0');
        const hh=String(d.getUTCHours()).padStart(2,'0'),mm=String(d.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');
        return `${y}-${mo}-${day}T${hh}:${mm}`;
      }
    }
  }
  const mm=raw.replace(' ','T').match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})T(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  return mm?`${mm[1]}-${String(mm[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(mm[3]).padStart(2,'0')}T${String(mm[4]).padStart(2,'0')}:${mm[5]}`:raw;
}

    lateEntry:String(row[9]||'').trim(),earlyExit:String(row[10]||'').trim(),reason:String(row[11]||'').trim(),
    priority:Number(row[12]||dormRulePriority(ro

function dormRuleDateValue(value){
  if(value===null||value===undefined||value==='')return '';
  const raw=String(value).trim();
  if(/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(raw)){
    const serial=Number(raw);
    if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000){
      const d=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+Math.round(serial*86400000));
      if(!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())){
        const y=d.getUTCFullYear(),mo=String(d.getUTCMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),day=String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2,'0');
        const hh=String(d.getUTCHours()).padStart(2,'0'),mm=String(d.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');
        return `${y}-${mo}-${day}T${hh}:${mm}`;
      }
    }
  }
  const mm=raw.replace(' ','T').match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})T(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  return mm?`${mm[1]}-${String(mm[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(mm[3]).padStart(2,'0')}T${String(mm[4]).padStart(2,'0')}:${mm[5]}`:raw;
}

  if(sheetRow>0)await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'31_지각기록'!A${sheetRow}:P${sheetRow}`,values); else await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'31_지각기록'!A:P",values);
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true,id,data:await fetchLiveData({force:true})};
}

  const key=String(id||"").trim(); if(!key)throw new Error("삭제할 지각 ID가 없습니다.");
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath()); if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account); const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'31_지각기록'!A3:P3000"]); const rows=(result?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);
  const pos=rows.findIndex(r=>String(r?.[0]||"").trim()===key); if(pos<0)throw new Error("삭제할 지각 기록을 찾지 못했습니다.");
  await clearSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'31_지각기록'!A${pos+4}:P${pos+4}`);liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true,data:await fetchLiveData({force:true})};

function dormRulePriority(category=""){
  return ({exception:50,closure:40,exam:30,vacation:25,base:20,period:10})[String(category||"")]||5;
}
function dormRuleRow(payload={},id,nowIso){
  return [[
    id,
    String(payload.category||""),
    String(payload.name||""),
    String(payload.start||""),
    String(payload.end||""),
    String(payload.days||"전체"),
    String(payload.effect||""),
    String(payload.entryTime||""),
    String(payload.exitTime||""),
    String(payload.lateEntry||""),
    String(payload.earlyExit||""),
    String(payload.reason||""),
    Number(payload.priority||dormRulePriority(payload.category)),
    payload.active===false?"N":"Y",
    String(payload.note||""),
    nowIso,
    String(payload.writer||"UEP"),
    String(payload.source||"UEP 규칙센터"),
    String(payload.operationState||""),
    String(payload.actualExit||""),
    String(payload.actualReentry||""),
    payload.priorNightAcademyHome?"Y":"N",
    payload.formRequired?"Y":"N",
    payload.individualOutingAllowed?"Y":"N"
  ]];
}
function dormRuleDateValue(value){
  if(value===null||value===undefined||value==='')return '';
  const raw=String(value).trim();
  if(/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(raw)){
    const serial=Number(raw);
    if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000){
      const d=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+Math.round(serial*86400000));
      if(!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())){
        const y=d.getUTCFullYear(),mo=String(d.getUTCMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),day=String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2,'0');
        const hh=String(d.getUTCHours()).padStart(2,'0'),mm=String(d.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');
        return `${y}-${mo}-${day}T${hh}:${mm}`;
      }
    }
  }
  const mm=raw.replace(' ','T').match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})T(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  return mm?`${mm[1]}-${String(mm[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(mm[3]).padStart(2,'0')}T${String(mm[4]).padStart(2,'0')}:${mm[5]}`:raw;
}
function dormRuleObjectFromRow(row=[]){
  return {
    id:String(row[0]||'').trim(),category:String(row[1]||'').trim(),name:String(row[2]||'').trim(),
    start:dormRuleDateValue(row[3]),end:dormRuleDateValue(row[4]),days:String(row[5]||'전체').trim(),
    effect:String(row[6]||'').trim(),entryTime:String(row[7]||'').trim(),exitTime:String(row[8]||'').trim(),
    lateEntry:String(row[9]||'').trim(),earlyExit:String(row[10]||'').trim(),reason:String(row[11]||'').trim(),
    priority:Number(row[12]||dormRulePriority(row[1])),active:String(row[13]||'Y').trim().toUpperCase()!=='N',
    note:String(row[14]||'').trim(),modifiedAt:String(row[15]||'').trim(),writer:String(row[16]||'').trim(),source:String(row[17]||'').trim(),
    operationState:String(row[18]||'').trim(),actualExit:dormRuleDateValue(row[19]),actualReentry:dormRuleDateValue(row[20]),
    priorNightAcademyHome:/^(Y|YES|TRUE|1)$/i.test(String(row[21]||'')),
    formRequired:/^(Y|YES|TRUE|1)$/i.test(String(row[22]||'')),
    individualOutingAllowed:/^(Y|YES|TRUE|1)$/i.test(String(row[23]||''))
  };
}
async function saveDormRule(payload={}){
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account), now=new Date().toISOString();
  const id=String(payload.id||`DORM-RULE-${crypto.randomUUID()}`).trim();
  const range="'40_학사외출규칙'!A3:X1000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
  const rows=matrix.slice(1);
  const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===id);
  const row=dormRuleRow(payload,id,now);
  if(pos>=0) await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'40_학사외출규칙'!A${pos+4}:X${pos+4}`,row);
  else await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'40_학사외출규칙'!A:X",row);
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  const record={...payload,id,priority:Number(payload.priority||dormRulePriority(payload.category)),active:payload.active!==false,modifiedAt:now,writer:String(payload.writer||"UEP"),source:String(payload.source||"UEP 규칙센터")};
  return {ok:true,id,record};
}
async function deleteDormRule(id=""){
  const key=String(id||"").trim(); if(!key) throw new Error("삭제할 학사외출 규칙 ID가 없습니다.");
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account), range="'40_학사외출규칙'!A3:X1000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const rows=(result?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);
  const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===key);
  if(pos<0) throw new Error("삭제할 학사외출 규칙을 시트에서 찾지 못했습니다.");
  await clearSheetValues(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'40_학사외출규칙'!A${pos+4}:X${pos+4}`);
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  return {ok:true,id:key};
}

  const token=await getSheetsToken(account), range="'40_학사외출규칙'!A3:X1000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const rows=(result?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);
  const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===key);
  if(pos<0) throw new Error("삭제할 학사외출 규칙을 시트에서 찾지 못했습니다.");
  await clearSheetValues(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'40_학사외출규칙'!A${pos+4}:X${pos+4}`);
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  return {ok:true,id:key};

async function saveDormOuting(payload={}){
  const allowedRoles=new Set(['admin','grade_head','grade_manager','homeroom']);
  if(payload.role&&!allowedRoles.has(String(payload.role)))throw new Error('학사외출 교사등록 권한이 없습니다.');
  const date=String(payload.date||'').slice(0,10),category=String(payload.category||'외출'),studentNo=String(payload.studentNo||'').replace(/\D/g,''),name=String(payload.name||'').trim();
  if(!date||!studentNo||!name)throw new Error('학생·학번·날짜 정보를 확인해 주세요.');
  const home=/퇴소|귀가/.test(category),outTime=String(payload.outTime||''),returnTime=String(payload.returnTime||'');
  if(!outTime)throw new Error('외출·귀가 시간을 입력해 주세요.');
  if(!home&&!returnTime)throw new Error('복귀 예정시간을 입력해 주세요.');
  const account=await resolveSchoolServiceAccount();if(!validateServiceAccount(account))throw new Error('학교 공용 Google 쓰기 연결을 찾지 못했습니다.');
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account),now=new Date().toISOString(),id=String(payload.id||`OUT-TEACHER-${crypto.randomUUID()}`),grade=String(payload.grade||studentNo.slice(0,1)||'1'),className=String(payload.className||studentNo.slice(1,2)||''),number=String(payload.number||studentNo.slice(-2)).replace(/^0/,'');
  const existing=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'02_학사외출_일자별'!A3:AD5000"]),matrix=existing?.[0]?.values||[],headers=(matrix[0]||[]).map(x=>String(x||'').trim()),col=(...names)=>names.map(n=>headers.indexOf(n)).find(i=>i>=0);
  const dcol=col('외출일자','신청일자','운영일','일자','날짜'),ncol=col('학번','학생학번'),idcol=col('학생ID','학생 ID'),ccol=col('외출구분','신청구분','구분'),tcol=col('외출시간','출발예정시간','출발시간');
  const duplicate=matrix.slice(1).some(r=>String(r[dcol]||'').slice(0,10)===date&&((ncol>=0&&String(r[ncol]||'').replace(/\.0$/,'')===studentNo)||(idcol>=0&&String(r[idcol]||'')===String(payload.studentId||'')))&&String(r[ccol]||'')===category&&String(r[tcol]||'')===outTime);
  if(duplicate)throw new Error('같은 학생의 동일한 학사외출 기록이 이미 있습니다.');
  const row=[[id,'',date,String(payload.studentId||''),grade,className,number,name,'교사등록',category,home?'귀가형':'복귀형',outTime,home?'':returnTime,home?returnTime:'',String(payload.reason||''),String(payload.destination||''),String(payload.guardian||''),String(payload.note||''),String(payload.status||'확인'),now,now,'UEP반영',now,String(payload.writer||''),'','','Y','교사 직접등록','담임·관리자 직접 확인',now]];
  await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'02_학사외출_일자별'!A:AD",row);
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;const data=await fetchLiveData({force:true});return {ok:true,id,data,record:{id,studentId:String(payload.studentId||''),studentNo,name,date,className,number,category,outTime,returnTime,reason:String(payload.reason||''),destination:String(payload.destination||''),note:String(payload.note||''),confirmed:String(payload.writer||''),status:'확인'}};

async function saveDormRule(payload={}){
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account), now=new Date().toISOString();
  const id=String(payload.id||`DORM-RULE-${crypto.randomUUID()}`).trim();
  const range="'40_학사외출규칙'!A3:X1000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
  const rows=matrix.slice(1);
  const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===id);
  const row=dormRuleRow(payload,id,now);
  if(pos>=0) await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'40_학사외출규칙'!A${pos+4}:X${pos+4}`,row);
  else await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'40_학사외출규칙'!A:X",row);
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  const record={...payload,id,priority:Number(payload.priority||dormRulePriority(payload.category)),active:payload.active!==false,modifiedAt:now,writer:String(payload.writer||"UEP"),source:String(payload.source||"UEP 규칙센터")};
  return {ok:true,id,record};
}

async function saveNightSupervisor(payload={}) {
  const allowedRoles=new Set(['admin','grade_head','grade_manager','homeroom','subject']);
  if(payload.role&&!allowedRoles.has(String(payload.role))) throw new Error('야자감독 수정 권한이 없습니다.');
  const id=String(payload.id||'').trim(), date=String(payload.date||'').slice(0,10), teacher=String(payload.teacher||'').trim();
  if(!teacher) throw new Error('변경할 감독교사가 없습니다.');
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error('Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.');
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
  const range="'43_야자감독계획'!A3:J1000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
  const headers=(matrix[0]||[]).map(v=>String(v||'').trim());
  const idCol=headers.indexOf('기록ID'), dateCol=headers.indexOf('운영일'), teacherCol=headers.indexOf('감독교사');
  if(teacherCol<0) throw new Error('43_야자감독계획의 감독교사 열을 찾지 못했습니다.');
  const rows=matrix.slice(1);
  const pos=rows.findIndex(row=> (id&&String(row?.[idCol]||'').trim()===id) || (!id&&String(row?.[dateCol]||'').slice(0,10)===date));
  if(pos<0) throw new Error('수정할 야자감독 일정을 찾지 못했습니다.');
  const sheetRow=pos+4;
  const colLetter=String.fromCharCode(65+teacherCol);
  await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'43_야자감독계획'!${colLetter}${sheetRow}:${colLetter}${sheetRow}`,[[teacher]]);
  liveDataCache=null; liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  const data=await fetchLiveData({force:true});
  return {ok:true,data};
}

async function saveNightSupervisor(payload={}) {
  const allowedRoles=new Set(['admin','grade_head','grade_manager','homeroom','subject']);
  if(payload.role&&!allowedRoles.has(String(payload.role))) throw new Error('야자감독 수정 권한이 없습니다.');
  const id=String(payload.id||'').trim(), date=String(payload.date||'').slice(0,10), teacher=String(payload.teacher||'').trim();
  if(!teacher) throw new Error('변경할 감독교사가 없습니다.');
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error('Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.');
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
  const range="'43_야자감독계획'!A3:J1000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
  const headers=(matrix[0]||[]).map(v=>String(v||'').trim());
  const idCol=headers.indexOf('기록ID'), dateCol=headers.indexOf('운영일'), teacherCol=headers.indexOf('감독교사');
  if(teacherCol<0) throw new Error('43_야자감독계획의 감독교사 열을 찾지 못했습니다.');
  const rows=matrix.slice(1);
  const pos=rows.findIndex(row=> (id&&String(row?.[idCol]||'').trim()===id) || (!id&&String(row?.[dateCol]||'').slice(0,10)===date));
  if(pos<0) throw new Error('수정할 야자감독 일정을 찾지 못했습니다.');
  const sheetRow=pos+4;
  const colLetter=String.fromCharCode(65+teacherCol);
  await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'43_야자감독계획'!${colLetter}${sheetRow}:${colLetter}${sheetRow}`,[[teacher]]);
  liveDataCache=null; liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  const data=await fetchLiveData({force:true});
  return {ok:true,data};
}

async function saveLunchDuty(payload={}) {
  const allowedRoles=new Set(['admin','grade_head','grade_manager','homeroom','subject']);
  if(payload.role&&!allowedRoles.has(String(payload.role))) throw new Error('급식지도 수정 권한이 없습니다.');
  const id=String(payload.id||'').trim();
  const date=String(payload.date||'').slice(0,10);
  const originalTeacher=String(payload.originalTeacher||payload.teacher||'').trim();
  const teacher=String(payload.teacher||'').trim();
  const dutyType=String(payload.dutyType||'급식실').trim();
  if(!teacher) throw new Error('변경할 급식지도 교사가 없습니다.');
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error('Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.');
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
  const range="'42_급식지도계획'!A3:K1000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
  const headers=(matrix[0]||[]).map(v=>String(v||'').trim());
  const idCol=headers.findIndex(h=>/기록ID|급식지도ID|^ID$/.test(h));
  const dateCol=headers.findIndex(h=>/운영일|일자|날짜/.test(h));
  const teacherCol=headers.findIndex(h=>/담당교사|지도교사|교사/.test(h));
  const typeCol=headers.findIndex(h=>/담당구분|구역|장소|역할/.test(h));
  if(teacherCol<0) throw new Error('42_급식지도계획의 담당교사 열을 찾지 못했습니다.');
  const rows=matrix.slice(1);
  const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>
    (id && idCol>=0 && String(row?.[idCol]||'').trim()===id) ||
    (!id && dateCol>=0 && String(row?.[dateCol]||'').slice(0,10)===date && String(row?.[teacherCol]||'').trim()===originalTeacher)
  );
  if(pos<0) throw new Error('수정할 급식지도 일정을 찾지 못했습니다.');
  const sheetRow=pos+4;
  const colLetter=(index)=>{let n=index+1,out='';while(n){n--;out=String.fromCharCode(65+n%26)+out;n=Math.floor(n/26)}return out};
  await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'42_급식지도계획'!${colLetter(teacherCol)}${sheetRow}:${colLetter(teacherCol)}${sheetRow}`,[[teacher]]);
  if(typeCol>=0) await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'42_급식지도계획'!${colLetter(typeCol)}${sheetRow}:${colLetter(typeCol)}${sheetRow}`,[[dutyType]]);
  liveDataCache=null; liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  const data=await fetchLiveData({force:true});
  return {ok:true,data};
}

async function saveInputCenterRecords(payload={}){
  const storageMode=String(payload.storageMode||'uep');
  let token=null;
  if(storageMode==='uep'){
    const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
    if(!validateServiceAccount(account))throw new Error('UEP 시트 영구 저장에는 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.');
    token=await getSheetsToken(account);
    if(!UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID) throw new Error('[UEP] 입력센터 원본 시트 연결정보가 없습니다.');
  }
  const now=new Date().toISOString(),batchId=String(payload.batchId||`IN-${Date.now()}`),year=String(new Date().getFullYear());
  const folder=path.join(inputCenterRoot(),batchId.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9가-힣_-]/g,'_'));await fs.mkdir(folder,{recursive:true});
  const prepared=[];let index=0;
  for(const record of Array.isArray(payload.records)?payload.records:[]){
    index++;let stored='';
    const pageSources=Array.isArray(record.pages)&&record.pages.length?record.pages.map(p=>String(p.filePath||'')).filter(Boolean):[String(record.filePath||'')].filter(Boolean);
    const storedPages=[];
    for(let pi=0;pi<pageSources.length;pi++){
      const sourcePath=pageSources[pi];
      try{const safeName=`${String(index).padStart(3,'0')}_p${pi+1}_${path.basename(sourcePath).replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g,'_')}`;const target=path.join(folder,safeName);await fs.copyFile(sourcePath,target);storedPages.push(path.relative(stableUserDataRoot(),target));}catch{}
    }
    stored=storedPages.join(' | ');
    const raw=String(record.text||'').slice(0,45000);const binary=!raw&&Boolean(record.extension);const id=`SRC-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
    const programId=String(record.programId||payload.programId||'').trim();
    const programKind=String(record.programKind||payload.programKind||'').trim();
    const programName=String(record.programName||payload.programName||'').trim();
    const reportFields=Array.isArray(record.reportFields)?record.reportFields:[];const routed=programId&&/보고서/.test(String(payload.type||''));
    const submittedAt=String(record.submittedAt||'').trim();
    const structured=JSON.stringify({kind:routed?'programReport':'inputSource',programId,programKind,programName,submittedAt,fields:reportFields.map(x=>({label:String(x.label||''),value:String(x.value||'')}))}).slice(0,45000);
    const note=routed?`ROUTE:PROGRAM_REPORT:${programId}`:(binary?'원본 보관 완료 · 텍스트 후처리 대상':'입력센터 원문 등록');
    const row=[id,batchId,String(payload.source||'교사 파일'),String(payload.type||'기타'),String(payload.subtype||''),year,String(payload.semester||'2'),String(record.studentId||''),String(record.studentNo||''),String(record.name||''),String(record.grade||''),String(record.className||''),String(record.fileName||''),stored,String(payload.title||record.fileName||''),raw,structured,binary?'텍스트확인필요':'원문등록',String(record.matchStatus||(record.studentNo?'교사확인':'확인필요')),programId,programKind,String(payload.teacher||''),now,note];
    prepared.push({row,studentId:String(record.studentId||''),studentNo:String(record.studentNo||''),programId,programKind,programName,submittedAt,routed});
  }
  let newItems=prepared.slice(),duplicateItems=[],updateItems=[];
  if(storageMode==='uep'&&prepared.some(x=>x.routed)){
    const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'02_자료원본'!A3:X10000"]);
    const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];const headers=(matrix[0]||[]).map(v=>String(v||'').trim());const col=(name)=>headers.indexOf(name);
    const existing=matrix.slice(1).map((r,i)=>{let meta={};try{meta=JSON.parse(String(r[col('구조화데이터')]||''));}catch{}return {sheetRow:i+4,row:r,inputId:String(r[col('입력ID')]||''),studentId:String(r[col('학생ID')]||''),studentNo:String(r[col('학번')]||''),programId:String(r[col('프로그램ID')]||meta.programId||''),submittedAt:String(meta.submittedAt||'').trim()};}).filter(x=>x.programId);
    newItems=[];
    for(const item of prepared){
      if(!item.routed){newItems.push(item);continue;}
      const same=existing.filter(x=>((item.studentId&&x.studentId===item.studentId)||(!item.studentId&&item.studentNo&&x.studentNo===item.studentNo))&&x.programId===item.programId);
      const exact=same.find(x=>x.submittedAt===item.submittedAt&&item.submittedAt);
      if(exact){duplicateItems.push({...item,existing:exact});continue;}
      if(same.length){const latest=same[same.length-1];item.row[0]=latest.inputId||item.row[0];updateItems.push({...item,existing:latest});continue;}
      newItems.push(item);
    }
    if(!payload.confirmed)return {ok:true,preflight:true,requiresConfirmation:true,count:prepared.length,newCount:newItems.length,duplicateCount:duplicateItems.length,updateCount:updateItems.length,skippedCount:duplicateItems.length,batchId,storageMode};
  }
  const manifest={batchId,source:String(payload.source||''),type:String(payload.type||''),subtype:String(payload.subtype||''),storageMode,title:String(payload.title||''),semester:String(payload.semester||''),teacher:String(payload.teacher||''),savedAt:now,records:prepared.map(x=>x.row),dedupe:{newCount:newItems.length,duplicateCount:duplicateItems.length,updateCount:updateItems.length}};
  await fs.writeFile(path.join(folder,'batch-manifest.json'),JSON.stringify(manifest,null,2),'utf8');
  if(storageMode==='uep'){
    const firstFile=prepared[0]?.row?.[12]||'';
    const batchRow=[batchId,now,String(payload.teacher||''),String(payload.source||''),String(firstFile||''),String(path.extname(String(firstFile||'')).replace(/^\./,'').toUpperCase()),1,prepared.length,String(payload.type||''),String(payload.subtype||''),year,String(payload.semester||''),String(payload.programId||prepared[0]?.programId||''),String(payload.programKind||prepared[0]?.programKind||''),String(payload.programName||prepared[0]?.programName||''),'저장완료',newItems.length+updateItems.length,`중복제외 ${duplicateItems.length}건`];
    await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'01_입력배치'!A:R",[batchRow]);
    if(newItems.length)await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'02_자료원본'!A:X",newItems.map(x=>x.row));
    for(const item of updateItems)await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'02_자료원본'!A${item.existing.sheetRow}:X${item.existing.sheetRow}`,[item.row]);
    const logRow=[`LOG-${crypto.randomUUID()}`,batchId,'','원본저장','입력센터','성공',prepared.length,newItems.length+updateItems.length,0,duplicateItems.length,0,now,new Date().toISOString(),String(payload.teacher||''),'',`[UEP] 입력센터 원본 저장`];
    await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'90_처리로그'!A:P",[logRow]);
  }
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  return {ok:true,count:newItems.length+updateItems.length,newCount:newItems.length,duplicateCount:duplicateItems.length,updateCount:updateItems.length,skippedCount:duplicateItems.length,batchId,storageMode,inputCenterSpreadsheetId:UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID};
}

function inputCenterNatureToStoredType(nature=""){
  const n=String(nature||"");
  if(n==="보고서")return "보고서·탐구보고서";
  if(n==="신청")return "프로그램 신청서";
  if(n==="만족도조사")return "만족도·설문조사";
  if(n==="출결·명단")return "출결·참여명단";
  return n||"기타";
}
function inputCenterProgramIdRaw(id=""){return String(id||"").replace(/^(common|selected)-/,'');}
function inputCenterSemester(value="",submittedAt=""){
  const t=String(value||""); if(/(^|[^0-9])1([^0-9]|$)|1학기/.test(t))return "1학기"; if(/(^|[^0-9])2([^0-9]|$)|2학기/.test(t))return "2학기";
  const m=String(submittedAt||"").match(/\d{4}[-/.](\d{1,2})/);return m&&Number(m[1])<=7?"1학기":"2학기";
}
async function classifyInputCenterBatch(payload={}){
  try{
    const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());if(!validateServiceAccount(account))throw new Error('Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.');
    const batchId=String(payload.batchId||'').trim();if(!batchId)throw new Error('입력 배치를 선택해 주세요.');
    const token=await getSheetsToken(account);const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'02_자료원본'!A3:X10000"]);const rows=(result?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);
    let count=0;const storedType=inputCenterNatureToStoredType(payload.nature);const pid=String(payload.programId||'');const pk=String(payload.programKind||'');const note=pid?`ROUTE:PROGRAM_REPORT:${pid}`:'';const writes=[];
    for(let i=0;i<rows.length;i++)if(String(rows[i]?.[1]||'')===batchId){const sheetRow=i+4;const current=Array.from({length:24},(_,j)=>rows[i]?.[j]??'');current[3]=storedType;current[4]=payload.nature==='보고서'?'보고서 제출':payload.nature;let meta={};try{meta=JSON.parse(String(current[16]||''));}catch{}current[6]=inputCenterSemester(payload.programSemester||'',meta.submittedAt||'');current[17]='검수완료';current[19]=pid;current[20]=pk;current[23]=note||current[23]||'';writes.push({range:`'02_자료원본'!A${sheetRow}:X${sheetRow}`,values:[current]});count++;}
    await batchUpdateSheetValues(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,writes);
    liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true,count,batchId};
  }catch(error){return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||String(error)};}
}
async function normalizeInputCenterBatch(payload={}){
  try{
    const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());if(!validateServiceAccount(account))throw new Error('Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.');
    const records=Array.isArray(payload.records)?payload.records:[];if(!records.length)throw new Error('정규화할 자료가 없습니다.');
    if(String(payload.nature||'')!=='보고서')throw new Error('현재 정규화 자동적용은 보고서부터 지원합니다. 신청·만족도·명단은 다음 라우팅 단계에서 연결합니다.');
    const kind=String(payload.programKind||'')==='selected'?'selected':'common';
    const targetSheet=kind==='selected'?'21_선택보고서_정규화':'20_공통보고서_정규화';
    const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
    // REPORT_TEXT_V2 표준 스키마 A:X. 원본유형/원본ID로 출처를 구분하므로 입력센터 자료도 기존 데이터 바로 다음 행에 연속 저장합니다.
    const DATA_START_ROW=4, INPUT_END_ROW=5000;
    const existingResult=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID,[`'${targetSheet}'!A3:X${INPUT_END_ROW}`]);
    const matrix=existingResult?.[0]?.values||[];
    const existing=matrix.slice(1).map((row,index)=>({row, sheetRow:index+4})).filter(x=>(x.row||[]).some(v=>String(v??'').trim()));
    const prefix=kind==='selected'?'ISR':'ICR';
    let maxInputId=0;for(const item of existing){const id=String(item.row?.[0]||'');if(!id.startsWith(prefix+'-'))continue;const n=Number(id.match(/(\d+)$/)?.[1]||0);if(n>maxInputId)maxInputId=n;}
    const programId=inputCenterProgramIdRaw(payload.programId||records[0]?.programId||'');
    const programName=String(payload.programName||'');
    if(!programId)throw new Error('보고서는 연결 프로그램ID가 필요합니다. 02 검수·분류에서 프로그램을 확정해 주세요.');
    const hashText=value=>crypto.createHash('sha256').update(String(value||''),'utf8').digest('hex');
    const canonicalExistingHash=row=>{
      const declared=String(row?.[23]||'').trim();if(declared)return declared;
      const normalized=String(row?.[12]||'').trim();
      return hashText([row?.[2]||row?.[3]||'',row?.[7]||'',normalized].map(v=>String(v||'').trim()).join('\n'));
    };
    const prepared=[];let next=maxInputId+1;
    for(const rec of records){
      let meta={};try{meta=JSON.parse(String(rec.normalizedText||''));}catch{}
      const submittedAt=String(meta.submittedAt||rec.createdAt||'');

ction inputCenterSemester(value="",submittedAt=""){
  const t=String(value||""); if(/(^|[^0-9])1([^0-9]|$)|1학기/.test(t))return "1학기"; if(/(^|[^0-9])2([^0-9]|$)|2학기/.test(t))return "2학기";
  const m=String(submittedAt||"").match(/\d{4}[-/.](\d{1,2})/);return m&&Number(m[1])<=7?"1학기":"2학기";
}
async function classifyInputCenterBatch(payload={}){
  try{
    const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());if(!validateServiceAccount(account))throw new Error('Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.');

    const token=await getSheetsToken(account);const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'02_자료원본'!A3:X10000"]);const rows=(result?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);
    let count=0;const storedType=inputCenterNatureToStoredType(payload.nature);const pid=String(payload.programId||'');const pk=String(payload.programKind||'');const note=pid?`ROUTE:PROGRAM_REPORT:${pid}`:'';const writes=[];
    for(let i=0;i<rows.length;i++)if(String(rows[i]?.[1]||'')===batchId){const sheetRow=i+4;const current=Array.from({length:24},(_,j)=>rows[i]?.[j]??'');current[3]=storedType;current[4]=payload.nature==='보고서'?'보고서 제출':payload.nature;let meta={};try{meta=JSON.parse(String(current[16]||''));}catch{}current[6]=inputCenterSemester(payload.programSemester||'',meta.submittedAt||'');current[17]='검수완료';current[19]=pid;current[20]=pk;current[23]=note||current[23]||'';writes.push({range:`'02_자료원본'!A${sheetRow}:X${sheetRow}`,values:[current]});count++;}

  }catch(error){return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||String(error)};}
}
async function normalizeInputCenterBatch(payload={}){
  try{
    const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());if(!validateServiceAccount(account))throw new Error('Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.');
    const records=Array.isArray(payload.records)?payload.records:[];if(!records.length)throw new Error('정규화할 자료가 없습니다.');
    if(String(payload.nature||'')!=='보고서')throw new Error('현재 정규화 자동적용은 보고서부터 지원합니다. 신청·만족도·명단은 다음 라우팅 단계에서 연결합니다.');
    const kind=String(payload.programKind||'')==='selected'?'selected':'common';
    const targetSheet=kind==='selected'?'21_선택보고서_정규화':'20_공통보고서_정규화';
    const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
    // REPORT_TEXT_V2 표준 스키마 A:X. 원본유형/원본ID로 출처를 구분하므로 입력센터 자료도 기존 데이터 바로 다음 행에 연속 저장합니다.
    const DATA_START_ROW=4, INPUT_END_ROW=5000;
    const existingResult=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID,[`'${targetSheet}'!A3:X${INPUT_END_ROW}`]);
    const matrix=existingResult?.[0]?.values||[];
    const existing=matrix.slice(1).map((row,index)=>({row, sheetRow:index+4})).filter(x=>(x.row||[]).some(v=>String(v??'').trim()));
    const prefix=kind==='selected'?'ISR':'ICR';
    let maxInputId=0;for(const item of existing){const id=String(item.row?.[0]||'');if(!id.startsWith(prefix+'-'))continue;const n=Number(id.match(/(\d+)$/)?.[1]||0);if(n>maxInputId)maxInputId=n;}
    const programId=inputCenterProgramIdRaw(payload.programId||records[0]?.programId||'');
    const programName=String(payload.programName||'');
    if(!programId)throw new Error('보고서는 연결 프로그램ID가 필요합니다. 02 검수·분류에서 프로그램을 확정해 주세요.');
    const hashText=value=>crypto.createHash('sha256').update(String(value||''),'utf8').digest('hex');
    const canonicalExistingHash=row=>{
      const declared=String(row?.[23]||'').trim();if(declared)return declared;
      const normalized=String(row?.[12]||'').trim();
      return hashText([row?.[2]||row?.[3]||'',row?.[7]||'',normalized].map(v=>String(v||'').trim()).join('\n'));
    };
    const prepared=[];let next=maxInputId+1;
    for(const rec of records){
      let meta={};try{meta=JSON.parse(String(rec.normalizedText||''));}catch{}
      const submittedAt=String(meta.submittedAt||rec.createdAt||'');
      const pairs=inputCenterReportPairs(meta);
      const reviewedContent=String(rec.reviewText||'').trim();
      const normalizedContent=reviewedContent||inputCenterReportText(meta)||String(rec.rawText||'').trim();
      if(!normalizedContent)continue;
      const signature=(Array.isArray(meta.fields)?meta.fields:[]).find(f=>/서명|동의/.test(String(f.label||'')))?.value||'';
      const semester=inputCenterSemester(payload.programSemester||'',submittedAt);
      const studentKey=String(rec.studentId||rec.studentNo||'').trim();
      const logicalKey=`${rec.schoolYear||'2026'}|${semester}|${studentKey}|${programId}`;
      const sourceType='INPUT_CENTER';
      const sourceId=String(rec.id||'').trim()||`INPUT-${hashText(`${rec.batchId||''}|${studentKey}|${submittedAt}`).slice(0,20)}`;
      const sourceBatchId=String(rec.batchId||payload.batchId||'').trim();
      const sourceModifiedAt=String(rec.createdAt||submittedAt||new Date().toISOString());
      const contentHash=hashText([studentKey,programId,normalizedContent].map(v=>String(v||'').trim()).join('\n'));
      const row=[
        `${prefix}-${String(next++).padStart(5,'0')}`,kind==='selected'?'선택활동':'공통활동',rec.studentId||'',rec.studentNo||'',rec.name||'',rec.schoolYear||'2026',semester,
        programId,programName||meta.programName||'',submittedAt,'Y',/^(O|Y|동의|예|동의함)$/i.test(String(signature).trim())?'동의함':'',
        normalizedContent,JSON.stringify(pairs),'REPORT_TEXT_V2',rec.id||'',rec.studentId?'정상':'확인필요',sourceType,sourceId,sourceBatchId,logicalKey,sourceModifiedAt,'정상',contentHash
      ];
      prepared.push({rec,row,sourceType,sourceId,logicalKey,contentHash,submittedAt});
    }
    if(!prepared.length)throw new Error('정규화할 보고서 본문을 찾지 못했습니다. 02 검수내용을 확인해 주세요.');
    let newCount=0,duplicateCount=0,updateCount=0;const actions=[];
    for(const p of prepared){
      const sameSource=existing.find(item=>String(item.row?.[17]||'')===p.sourceType&&String(item.row?.[18]||'')===p.sourceId);
      if(sameSource){
        const old=sameSource.row;
        if(String(old?.[22]||'정상')!=='삭제됨'&&canonicalExistingHash(old)===p.contentHash){duplicateCount++;actions.push({type:'duplicate',row:p.row,rec:p.rec});continue;}
        p.row[0]=old?.[0]||p.row[0];updateCount++;actions.push({type:'update',row:p.row,rec:p.rec,sheetRow:sameSource.sheetRow});continue;
      }
      const sameContent=existing.find(item=>{
        const r=item.row;if(String(r?.[22]||'정상')==='삭제됨')return false;
        return String(r?.[20]||'')===p.logicalKey&&canonicalExistingHash(r)===p.contentHash;
      });
      if(sameContent){duplicateCount++;actions.push({type:'duplicate',row:p.row,rec:p.rec});continue;}
      newCount++;actions.push({type:'new',row:p.row,rec:p.rec});
    }
    if(!payload.confirmed)return {ok:true,requiresConfirmation:true,targetSheet,programName,newCount,duplicateCount,updateCount,errorCount:0};
    // 기존 입력센터 제출 갱신도 한 번의 batchUpdate로 처리하여 Write quota를 소모하지 않습니다.
    const reportUpdates=actions.filter(a=>a.type==='update').map(a=>({range:`'${targetSheet}'!A${a.sheetRow}:X${a.sheetRow}`,values:[a.row]}));
    await batchUpdateSheetValues(token,UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID,reportUpdates);
    // 신규 입력센터 정규화는 출처별 물리 구역을 나누지 않고 실제 마지막 데이터 행 바로 다음에 연속 저장합니다.
    const lastUsed=existing.length?Math.max(...existing.map(item=>item.sheetRow)):DATA_START_ROW-1;
    const news=actions.filter(a=>a.type==='new');
    if(news.length){
      const start=Math.max(DATA_START_ROW,lastUsed+1),end=start+news.length-1;
      if(end>INPUT_END_ROW)throw new Error(`보고서 정규화 시트의 저장 가능 행을 초과했습니다. (${DATA_START_ROW}~${INPUT_END_ROW}행)`);
      await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'${targetSheet}'!A${start}:X${end}`,news.map(a=>a.row));
    }
    // 입력센터 원본에서 삭제된 자료는 정규화 원장에서 상태만 '삭제됨'으로 남겨 이력을 보존합니다.
    const srcResult=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'02_자료원본'!A3:X10000"]);
    const src=(srcResult?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);const liveSourceIds=new Set(src.map(r=>String(r?.[0]||'')).filter(Boolean));
    const deleteWrites=[];
    for(const item of existing){
      const row=item.row;if(String(row?.[17]||'')!=='INPUT_CENTER')continue;
      const sid=String(row?.[18]||'');if(!sid||liveSourceIds.has(sid)||String(row?.[22]||'')==='삭제됨')continue;
      deleteWrites.push({range:`'${targetSheet}'!W${item.sheetRow}:W${item.sheetRow}`,values:[['삭제됨']]});
    }
    await batchUpdateSheetValues(token,UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID,deleteWrites);

    // v0.80.45: 쓰기 응답만 믿지 않고 목적지 원장을 다시 읽어 실제 저장을 검증합니다.
    // 같은 원본ID가 있거나, 동일 논리키+내용해시가 실제로 존재해야만 해당 원본을 완료 처리합니다.
    const verifyResult=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID,[`'${targetSheet}'!A3:X${INPUT_END_ROW}`]);
    const verifyRows=(verifyResult?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1).filter(row=>(row||[]).some(v=>String(v??'').trim()));
    const actionVerified=a=>verifyRows.some(r=>{
      if(String(r?.[22]||'정상')
